STATE BANK OF INDIA 
LOCAL HEAD OFFICE 


New Delhi-110001, the 9th February 1983 


NOTICE 


1. Shri Devender Kapoor, Officer Senior Management 
Grade Scale IV A assumed charge as Chief Manager, 
New Delhi Main Branch as at the close of business 
on 30-4-1982 vice Shri B, L. Chadha. 


2. Shri Priya Brat, Officer Senior Management Grade 
Scale V assumed charge as Chief Manager, New Delhi 
Main Branch as at the close of business on 18-12-1982. 


3. Shri Radhey Shyam, Officer Sr, Management Grade 
Scale IV assumed charge as Manager (Accounts) at 
New Delhi Main Branch as at the close of business 
on 12-10-1981 vice Shri O. P. Verma. 


4. Shri O. P. Verma, Officer Senior Management Grade 
Scale-IV assumed charge as Manager (Accounts) at 
New Delhi Main Branch as at the close of busincsi 
on 11-8-1982. 


5. Shri R. C. Agarwala, Office Senior Management 
Grade,Scale IV assumed charge as Manager (Foreign 
Exchange) at New Delhi Main Branch as at the close • 
of business on 26-8-1982. 


6. Shri I. C. Luthra, Officer Senior Management Grade 
Scale IV A assumed charge as Manager (C&I) at New 
Delhi Main Branch as at the close of business on 
1-1-1982. 


7. Shri Madhav Mhole, Officer Middle Management 
Grade Scale III assumed charge as Manager (PBD) at 
Chandni Chowk (Delhi) Branch, as at the close of 
business on 4-11-1982. 


8. Shri Y. P. Sehgal, Officer Senior Management Grade 
Scale IV A assumed charge as Manager (Exports) at 
Overseas Branch, New Delhi as at the close of busi¬ 
ness on 5-4-1982, 


9. Shri S. M, Chopra, Officer Senior Management Grade 
Scale IV assumed charge as Manager (Public Sector) 
at Overseas Branch, New Delhi as at the close of 
business on 21-8-1982, 


10. Shri G. L. Malhotra, Officer Middle Management 
Grade Scale III assumed charge as Manager (Services) 
at Overseas Branch, New Delhi as at the close of 
business on 7-9-1982. 


11. Shri R. N. Bajaj, Officer Middle Management Grade 
Scale III assumed charge as Superintendent (Securities 
Department) at New Delhi Main Branch as at the 
close of business on 8-1-1983. 


B. D. DIKSHIT 
Chief General Manager 


EMPLOYEES’ STATE INSURANCE CORPORATION 
BANGALORE 


No. 53, V. 34. 13-5-81. Coord.—-In partial modification of 
this office Notification published in Part III Section-4 of the 
Gazette of India, dated 4-7-81, it is hereby notified that Shri 
L. Nagaraju, Labour Welfare Officer, KSRTC, Regional Work¬ 
shop, Hubli has been nominated as a member of the Local 
Committee, Hubli area, in place of Shri R. K. Betkeri, under 
Regulation 10-A(1) (d) of the ESI (General) Regulations, 


No. 53, V. 34. 13-5-81, Coord.—In partial modification of 
this office Notification published in Pari III Sectlon-4 of the 
Gazette of India dated 22-5-82. it Is hereby notified that Shri 
B. S. Rndhakrishna, Senior Labour Officer, Sujatha Textile 
Mills, Nanjangud, has been nominated as a member of the 
Local Committee, Nanjangud area, in place of Shri M. S. 
Sham, under Regulation 10-A(1) (d) of the ESI (General) 
Regulations, 1950. 


No, 53, V. 34. 13-5-81. Coord.—In partial modification of 
this office Notification published in Part In Section-4 of the 
Gazette of India dated 4-7181, it is hereby notified that Shri 
V. H. Raghavendra, Deputy Manager, Personnel, Sonalkar 
Tools Works Pvt. Ltd., Harihar, has been nominated as a 
member of the .Local Committee, Harihar area in place of 
Shri M, Keshava Murthy, under Regulation 10-A(l)(d) of 
the ESI (General) Regulations 1950. 


By order. 


B. K. RAMACHANDRA RAO
